  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测需要文本、视觉与上下文证据，尤其当多张图像提供互补线索时；MLLM生成的辅助信息常混杂有用背景知识与冗余视觉描述，需对其生成字段进行角色分工与筛选。

Method: SEAF为双分支框架：语义分支融合原文、冻结MLLM生成的实体背景与OCR文本；视觉分支结合全局与局部视觉特征及跨模态、跨图像相似度统计；生成字段被分派不同角色（实体背景用于语义输入，图像描述提供对齐统计）；并设计文本锚定平滑聚合（TASA），按图像与原帖文本相似度加权，同时保留字面相似度较低图像的贡献，最后相加两分支分类得分。

Result: 在MMSD3.0多图基准上，SEAF优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 对MLLM生成信息进行结构化角色划分与选择性利用，并结合文本锚定平滑的视觉证据聚合，可有效提升多图多模态讽刺检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires textual, visual, and contextual evidence, particularly when multiple images provide complementary cues. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate auxiliary information from a post's text and images, but their outputs may mix useful background knowledge with redundant visual descriptions. We propose SEAF, a dual-branch framework for selective semantic enrichment and visual evidence fusion. SEAF assigns generated fields to distinct roles: entity background enriches semantic input, while image descriptions supply alignment statistics. The semantic branch combines the original post text, background from a frozen MLLM, and optical character recognition (OCR) text. The aligned visual branch combines global and local visual features with statistics of cross-modal and inter-image similarity. Text-Anchored Smoothed Aggregation (TASA) weights images by their similarity to the original post text while preserving contributions from images with low literal similarity. Summing the branch classification scores integrates contextual semantics with visual evidence. Experiments on the multi-image MMSD3.0 benchmark show that SEAF outperforms all compared methods.

</details>


### [72] [Minimal Memory for Correctable Visual Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25675)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Coherent Archive Memory (CAM)，用可精确更新的几何摘要保存历史存在性约束，在"稳定观测+有界分歧"下给出后验纠错所需比特的稀疏/稠密切配界（一维对数、高维多项式开销），并以实验验证联合见证在摄影图像风险上的严格改进及对遮挡位置的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉延续需从单一演化场景状态生成未来帧，该状态必须可修订：当物体重现或后视角改变先前解释时，若不能重放历史图像，仅够当前渲染的摘要会丢失纠错所需候选。

Method: 建立稳定观测与有界分歧假设下后验纠错比特需求的稀疏与稠密匹配界；设计CAM：以裁剪膨胀与坐标赋值实现精确更新的几何摘要存储，再用历史存在性约束为全部请求帧选取单一状态。

Result: 静态流在给定平滑度与间隔尺度下，纠错至少需要一维对数、高维多项式级额外开销；穷举摄影测试显示联合见证带来严格的最坏真值图像风险增益；受控神经延续能恢复当前视图隐藏的位置。

Conclusion: 可修订状态的记忆开销具有可刻画的理论下界，且通过保留存在性约束的几何摘要可在实践中实现无损纠错与遮挡恢复。

Abstract: Given past images and known actions, visual continuation generates future frames from one evolving scene state. Keeping this state revisable matters when an object reappears or a later view changes an earlier interpretation. Once past images cannot be replayed, a summary sufficient for current rendering can lose candidates needed after correction. We establish matching sparse and dense bounds on the bits needed for later correction under stable observations and bounded disagreement. For static streams at the stated smoothness and margin scales, correction incurs at least logarithmic overhead in one dimension and polynomial overhead in higher dimensions. Coherent Archive Memory (CAM) stores geometric summaries with exact updates under clipped expansion and coordinate assignments, then uses historical existence constraints to select one state for all requested frames. Exhaustive photographic tests show strict worst-truth image-risk gains from joint witnesses, while controlled neural continuations recover positions hidden in the current view.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Entity Recognition: Vision-Centric Knowledge Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25680)
*Jiatong Ma,Yuchen Liu,Longteng Guo,Yichen Yuan,Yuze Wu,Dongze Hao,Ru Zhen,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VC-VQA基准，从视觉信息来源（内部/外部图像）与介入阶段（检索前/后）显式建模视觉信息在知识推理中的作用，并评估21个MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多将视觉信息仅视为实体识别手段、依赖文本外部知识，未能刻画视觉信息在多阶段推理中的真实角色，缺乏对细粒度视觉知识获取与复杂多阶段推理的系统评测。

Method: 构建大规模以视觉为中心的知识推理基准：按视觉信息来源（internal vs. external images）与介入阶段（pre-retrieval vs. post-retrieval）建模任务，要求模型检索外部视觉证据、检索前识别视觉线索、并对检索知识进行定位/验证/与内部图像匹配；同时构建可控多模态知识库，为每个样本提供可追溯的文本与视觉证据；在四种设置下评测21个代表性MLLM（无外部知识、给定证据、知识库检索、开放网络搜索）。

Result: 实验得出三点发现：当前模型在以视觉为中心的知识推理上仍表现不佳；细粒度视觉知识获取是主要瓶颈；智能体式检索凸显多步规划与跨模态知识整合的价值。

Conclusion: VC-VQA为评估MLLM的以视觉为中心知识推理提供了更具挑战性的基准，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: We propose VC-VQA, a large-scale benchmark for vision-centric knowledge reasoning, aiming to systematically evaluate the capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition and complex multi-stage reasoning. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily treat visual information as entity recognition and rely on text-based external knowledge, VC-VQA explicitly models the role of visual information by considering both its source (internal vs. external images) and stage of involvement (pre-retrieval vs. post-retrieval). Specifically, VC-VQA requires models to retrieve external visual evidence, identify visual clues before retrieval, and localize, verify, or match retrieved knowledge against the internal image. We further construct a controlled multimodal knowledge base with traceable textual and visual evidence for each sample. We evaluate 21 representative MLLMs under four settings: no external knowledge, with provided evidence, knowledge base retrieval, and open-web search. Experimental results lead to three key findings: current models still struggle with vision-centric knowledge reasoning; fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition remains the primary bottleneck; and agentic retrieval highlights the value of multi-step planning and cross-modal knowledge integration. Overall, VC-VQA provides a more challenging benchmark for evaluating vision-centric knowledge reasoning in MLLMs. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/CASIA-IVA-Lab/VC-VQA.

</details>


### [74] [D3-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Models with Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25689)
*Yanbo Li,An Xiang,Xiaoxin Hu,Feng Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Kejiang Ye,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D3-Cache将动态模态分解(DMD)用于扩散采样加速，对近期精确速度历史拟合DMD并仅预测调度器所消费的速度，配合horizon-aware门控决定何时跳过网络评估，无需训练或逐模型校准即可在四个图像/视频扩散Transformer上实现2.6–3.8倍加速且保持高保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer生成质量高，但每个采样步都需完整网络前向，代价高昂。免训练缓存虽可复用或预测网络输出以降低成本，但现有方法依赖逐模型校准的跳过准则，或需维护逐层预测历史，通用性与开销受限。

Method: 提出D3-Cache：对短窗口内的精确速度历史拟合动态模态分解(DMD)，仅对调度器实际消费的速度进行预测；引入horizon-aware门控——每次真实网络评估时将该步输出与预测值比较，误差越大则越快触发下一次评估，并对连续跳过步数设上限。方法无需训练、无需逐模型离线校准曲线，额外内存可忽略，且四个模型共享同一历史长度与风险容忍度超参数。

Result: 在四个先进图像与视频模型上取得2.6–3.8倍加速，同时与基础模型输出保持高保真度。

Conclusion: D3-Cache以线性动力学预测加自适应门控实现免训练、免校准、低内存的扩散采样缓存加速，跨模型共享超参数体现出良好通用性，可在保证生成质量的前提下显著降低采样开销。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images and videos, but every sampling step requires a full network evaluation. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reusing or forecasting network outputs across steps, but common approaches use per-model calibrated skip criteria or maintain per-layer prediction histories. We present D3-Cache, which applies Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) to Diffusion sampling: it fits DMD to a short history of exact velocities and forecasts only the velocity that the scheduler consumes. A horizon-aware gate decides when to skip: whenever the network is evaluated, its output is compared with the forecast for that step, and the larger the error, the sooner the network is evaluated again, up to a cap on consecutive skipped steps. D3-Cache requires no training or per-model offline calibration curve and adds negligible memory. It shares one history length and risk tolerance across the four evaluated models. On four state-of-the-art image and video models, D3-Cache achieves speedups of 2.6-3.8x with high fidelity to the base model output.

</details>


### [75] [LIFT: Lifting Language-Controlled Image Fusion to Free-Viewpoint 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25696)
*Jingning Tian,Hao Zhang,Yumin Huang,Zizhuo Li,Xingyu Jiang,Erdun Zhao,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语言控制的3D图像融合范式L3D-IF及框架LIFT，将多模态融合从2D扩展到自由视点3D场景，实现语言引导、跨视点一致的融合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态图像融合仅从特定视点的配对观测生成单张融合图像，难以实现全面场景感知，也难以适配多样化感知目标。作者希望将融合扩展到自由视点3D场景，并由语言指令引导。

Method: LIFT包含三部分：(1) Semantic Modality Binding（SMB），解析语言指令识别语义目标及其模态偏好，并绑定到共享高斯场景的对应区域，建立持久的语义—空间对应以支持跨视点一致的融合控制；(2) Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance（LCGA），通过自适应多模态混合与语言/视点条件化的外观细化，实现全场景多模态外观建模，在保持纯模态端点源保真度的同时实现空间自适应融合外观；(3) 构建Multi-View Language-Supervision（MVLS）数据集，将语言指令与标定视点关联到调制后的融合图像，为从有限多视点观测学习指令相关融合提供监督。

Result: 实验表明LIFT能在连续视点下实现连贯的多模态融合，准确进行语义目标定位，并在多样语言指令下实现一致的区域融合控制。

Conclusion: LIFT将多模态图像融合推广至语言控制的三维自由视点场景，验证了语义绑定+高斯外观建模+多视图语言监督这一技术路线的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion traditionally generates a single fused image from paired observations at a specific viewpoint, limiting comprehensive scene perception and adaptability to diverse perceptual objectives. To overcome these limitations, we present LIFT, a novel framework for our proposed language-controlled 3D image fusion (L3D-IF) paradigm that extends multimodal image fusion from 2D images to free-viewpoint 3D scenes, enabling language-guided fusion tailored to diverse perceptual objectives. First, LIFT formulates Semantic Modality Binding (SMB), which interprets language instructions to identify semantic targets and their associated modality preferences and grounds these targets in corresponding regions of a shared Gaussian scene, establishing persistent semantic-to-spatial correspondences for consistent fusion control across viewpoints. Second, Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance (LCGA) is developed to achieve scene-wide multimodal appearance modeling by combining adaptive multimodal blending with language- and viewpoint-conditioned appearance refinement, enabling spatially adaptive realization of diverse fusion appearances while preserving source fidelity at pure-modality endpoints. Finally, we construct a Multi-View Language-Supervision (MVLS) dataset that associates language instructions and calibrated viewpoints with modulated fused images, providing effective supervision for learning instruction-dependent fusion from finite multi-view observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LIFT achieves coherent multimodal fusion across continuous viewpoints, accurate semantic target localization, and consistent regional fusion control under diverse language instructions.

</details>


### [76] [Slide2PPT: Native PowerPoint Reconstruction with Localization-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25708)
*Baoliang Chen,Xinlong Bu,Lingyu Zhu,Xiangjie Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的 Slide2PPT 框架，通过"类型化对象记录+固定渲染器"把图像转可编辑 PPTX，并引入 Slide2PPT-Bench 评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像转幻灯片需同时恢复视觉外观与原生对象结构以供后续编辑；视觉上还原的图表/表格可能缺乏可编辑的系列与单元格，而直接生成代码会把内容预测与渲染实现耦合。

Method: 统一类型化接口：模型仅预测对象记录，固定渲染器物化其原生属性；本地化引导的迭代精修将视觉差异映射到对象记录进行修正；支持区域更新（保留未选记录）与整页更新（处理跨区域关系）、记录级精修与目标对象编辑，由渲染反馈驱动迭代接受。无需任务特定训练或架构改动，兼容开源/闭源 VLM 骨干。

Result: 在开源与闭源骨干上，PPTX 生成比直接代码生成更可靠；科学图形上的布局与文本恢复提升；超越几何图元，提供原生图表与表格接口。构建 Slide2PPT-Bench，覆盖受控元素、真实幻灯片与科学图形，分别评测文件产出、渲染一致性与原生对象可用性。

Conclusion: 解耦内容预测与渲染实现、配合渲染反馈驱动的精修，是提升图像到原生可编辑 PPT 重建可靠性的有效路径，且可即插即用地迁移到多种骨干模型。

Abstract: Image-to-slide reconstruction must recover both visual appearance and native object structure for subsequent editing. Yet visually faithful charts and tables may lack editable series and cells, and direct code generation couples content prediction to rendering implementation. We present Slide2PPT, a model-agnostic, training-free framework that equips both open- and closed-source vision-language models with native PowerPoint reconstruction capabilities. Through a unified typed interface, the model predicts object records while a fixed renderer materializes their native properties, enabling integration with different backbones without task-specific training or architectural changes. Localization-guided refinement then connects visual discrepancies to object records for correction. Regional updates preserve unselected records, while whole-slide updates address cross-region relationships. Record refinement and targeted object editing support structural and attribute-level changes, with rendered feedback governing iterative acceptance. To evaluate this framework across diverse reconstruction demands, we introduce Slide2PPT-Bench, covering controlled elements, real slides, and scientific figures. Evaluation separately measures file production, rendered agreement, and native-object availability. Experiments across open- and closed-source backbones demonstrate substantially more reliable PPTX generation than direct code generation, improved layout and text recovery on scientific figures, and native chart and table interfaces beyond geometric primitives.

</details>


### [77] [Calibrate in Context: Efficient Data-Free Quantization from Small Synthetic Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25711)
*Haowen Hua,Sen Lin,Yaokun Huang,Zeyi Shao,Hao Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过改进图像合成与学生网络准备，从少量合成图像池实现准确的低比特恢复；提出FGC与渐进式反演，在18个W4A4/W5A5设置上超越MimiQ，256张图像即可快速达到DeiT-S/ViT-S 68.20%/66.90% Top-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型合成图像池下低位宽(如W4A4)恢复精度不足：局部响应误差更小的网格反而在学生网络的块上下文(block context)中引入更大误差，且现有方法(如MimiQ)在极少图像预算下成本高、精度有限。

Method: 两点改进：(1) Functional Grid Calibration (FGC)：基于学生网络当前块输入构造的全精度响应，选择激活尺度与整数偏移；校准期间权重固定，仅在消费算子处评估候选，以接近局部打分的代价将块上下文引入校准。(2) 渐进式反演(progressive inversion)：用逐步加深的教师前缀更新同一批图像，用于DeiT和ViT。

Result: 1000张图像下，完整流程在九个骨干网络的18个W4A4/W5A5设置中全部超过MimiQ报告精度；256张图像下，DeiT-S与ViT-S在W4A4达到ImageNet Top-1 68.20%/66.90%，单张RTX 5090耗时26–27分钟；相比强化的1K图像MimiQ提升13.03与24.36个百分点，且准备成本更低、恢复更新次数相同；低预算检查点在ImageNetV2上保持优势。

Conclusion: 结合FGC与渐进式反演的流水线能以极低图像预算和计算成本实现高精度低比特量化恢复，并具备跨骨干与跨数据集的泛化性。

Abstract: We enable accurate low-bit recovery from small synthetic pools by improving both image synthesis and student preparation. A grid with lower local response error can incur greater error in the student's block context. Functional Grid Calibration (FGC) selects activation scales and integer offsets against full-precision responses constructed from the student's current block input. With weights fixed during calibration, FGC evaluates candidates only at their consuming operators, bringing block context to calibration at nearly local-scoring cost. For DeiT and ViT, progressive inversion updates the same images through increasingly deep teacher prefixes. With 1,000 images, the complete pipeline exceeds MimiQ's reported accuracy in all 18 W4A4/W5A5 settings across nine backbones. With 256 images, DeiT-S and ViT-S reach 68.20% and 66.90% ImageNet top-1 accuracy at W4A4 in 26-27 minutes on one RTX 5090. The pipeline outperforms strengthened 1K-image MimiQ by 13.03 and 24.36 percentage points at lower preparation cost and the same recovery-update count. The same low-budget checkpoints retain their advantage on ImageNetV2.

</details>


### [78] [SkillLens: Action-Guided Dense Visual Dynamics for Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25712)
*Deokhyun Ahn,Hyukjin Kim,Jae-Ho Han,Bumsub Ham,Heeseung Choi,Ig-Jae Kim,Haksub Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillLens利用原子动作描述作为时间引导，定位与熟练度相关的窗口并提取密集视觉动态特征，结合高低原型边界打分与珊瑚序数校准，融合自我/第三人称与注视线索，在Ego-Exo4D上达到0.542准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熟练度是高层且主观的属性，无法仅由动作身份刻画；现有方法虽利用自我-第三人称视频、注视、姿态、惯性等多模态观测，但传感器级证据与抽象熟练度标签之间存在难以弥合的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SkillLens框架：以原子动作描述作为时间引导（而非直接语义监督）定位熟练度相关窗口，进行密集视觉动态提取；每个窗口汇总为动作动态特征，并依据同活动-动作类别中更高/更低熟练度执行样本构成的高低原型边界进行打分；再将自我/第三人称PDM线索与全局“自我-第三视角-注视”基线通过CORAL序数校准融合。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D数据集上取得0.542的准确率。

Conclusion: 以原子动作描述作弱时间引导、配合原型边界打分与序数校准，可有效桥接传感器证据与抽象熟练度标签，验证了引导式局部动态建模在熟练度估计中的有效性。

Abstract: Human proficiency estimation is challenging because skill is a high-level and often subjective property that is not captured by action identity alone. Existing approaches leverage multimodal observations such as ego-exo video, gaze, pose, and inertial signals, but the gap between sensor-level evidence and abstract proficiency labels remains difficult to bridge. We present SkillLens, a proficiency estimation framework that uses atomic action descriptions as temporal guidance, rather than direct semantic supervision, to localize proficiency-relevant windows for dense visual-dynamics extraction. Each window is summarized into an action-dynamics feature and scored by a high-low prototype margin against higher- and lower-proficiency executions from the same activity-action category. Fusing these ego/exo PDM cues with a global ego-exo-gaze baseline through CORAL ordinal calibration, our method achieves 0.542 accuracy on Ego-Exo4D. Our project page is available at https://skill-lens.net/

</details>


### [79] [Interpreting DeepStack-Style Vision-Language Models: Do Input or Deeper-Layer Visual Tokens Drive Answers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25713)
*Yucheng Du,Yi Nian,Ruixue Lian,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过"冲突输入"探针方法研究DeepStack类视觉语言模型中后期视觉注入（later entries）的作用：在照片上原始DeepStack依赖后期条目而Qwen3-VL依赖主入口；削弱主入口会增强后期特征影响；后期特征可部分纠正错误；据此可设计按精度分层的分裂推理，节省49%传输字节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepStack式VLM在输入层（主入口）和深层（后期条目）重复注入视觉token，但当模型已拥有图像时，后期条目究竟贡献了什么尚不清楚。作者希望厘清多入口视觉信息的功能分工及其可利用性。

Method: 构造两个只在单个物体颜色上不同的图像版本，观察模型答案跟随哪一个条目（追随性探针）；在合成图像上仅降低主入口颜色饱和度，检验后期特征对两色相对分数的影响是否增强；同时退化两个入口并仅恢复后期条目，测试后期特征能否纠错；在GQA属性问题上做留一入口（zeroed entry）测试以预测模型可承受的条目损失；最后实施分裂推理实验，主入口以更高精度传输。

Result: 照片上原始DeepStack跟随后期条目，Qwen3-VL-8B/32B跟随主入口；合成图像上降低主入口饱和度后，四个模型（含Granite Vision）的后期特征对颜色相对分数影响均增强，即使在无冲突准确率仍为100%的轻微去饱和下也成立；条纹方向上模糊使两个Qwen模型掉50个百分点，32B恢复48点、8B仅恢复8点；单入口干净时的准确率能预测模型可失去哪个条目，6项预测中5项在新GQA属性问题上成立；仅保留主入口时Qwen3-VL-8B只掉0.3点，DeepStack任一条目单独最多掉3.45点；分裂推理中仅以更高精度发送主入口特征，相比所有条目统一精度减少49%字节且准确率相近。

Conclusion: 后期条目并非冗余：它携带可独立影响答案的颜色等信息，并在主入口质量下降或双入口退化时发挥作用，但其影响力受主入口强度调制；不同模型对各条目的依赖程度不同且可预测，这为按条目差异化分配精度与带宽的分裂推理提供了依据。

Abstract: DeepStack-style vision-language models feed visual tokens to the language model at its input, and again at deeper layers. We call these the main entry and the later entries. What do the later entries add when the model already has the image? We give the entries versions that differ in one object's color and see which one the answer follows. On photographs, the original DeepStack follows the later entries, while Qwen3-VL-8B/32B follow the main entry. Does weakening the main entry give the later features more influence? On synthetic images, we keep this conflict and reduce color saturation at the main entry only. The unchanged later features gain a stronger effect on the relative scores of the two colors, in all four models, including Granite Vision. This holds even under mild desaturation, when accuracy without the conflict stays at 100%. To test whether later features can also correct errors, we degrade both entries and restore only the later ones. The same blur costs both Qwen models 50 percentage points of accuracy on stripe orientation. The 32B model recovers 48 points and the 8B model only 8. Accuracy with one clean entry predicted which entry each model can lose, and five of six tested predictions held on new GQA attribute questions. With the other entry zeroed, Qwen3-VL-8B loses only 0.3 points on its main entry alone, and DeepStack at most 3.45 points on either entry alone. In split inference on these questions, DeepStack can then send only its main-entry features at higher precision. This takes 49% fewer bytes than all entries at a shared precision, at similar accuracy.

</details>


### [80] [SteerTrace: Tracing How Target Attacks Steer World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25719)
*Yukun Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SteerTrace：一种对世界动作模型（WAM）的通用目标攻击，并可在模型内部追踪其作用来源（future tokens、current-frame tokens、FFN 神经元、残差方向）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 同时预测未来视频与动作，其“想象未来”常被视为安全信号；需要检验该信号能否作为可靠安全依据，即攻击是否可绕过它并精确操控动作。

Method: 对每个模型学习一个 ℓ∞ 有界（4/255）扰动，跨帧、任务、相机视角共享，将执行动作推向攻击者指定目标；通过干预实验（恢复 future tokens / current-frame tokens、恢复 FFN 神经元、移除残差方向）以有符号目标效应量化各组件贡献。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 上、4 个家族共 8 个 WAM（FastWAM、Faster-WAM、OpenWAM、LingBot-VA）中，任务成功率降至近零且动作向目标偏移；同预算随机噪声与 BadWAM 几乎无目标方向偏移。标准攻击下，恢复 future tokens 消除 61-94% 效应（5 个动作专家关注其的模型）；若约束未来预测接近干净预测，效应转由 current-frame tokens 承载，恢复后消除 87-99%（对比 future tokens 的 9-15%）；仅恢复视频专家第 10-29 层 0.36% 的 FFN 神经元即消除 72-96%；残差方向 2-16 仅占 11-18% 偏移能量却可消除大部分效应。

Conclusion: WAM 的想象未来并非稳健安全信号：微小通用扰动即可大幅操控动作，且效应可通过改变约束条件在帧级 token 与稀疏 FFN 子空间中迁移；对 WAM 安全评估与防御需同时考虑动作通路与视频骨干的内部表征。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict future videos and actions with one backbone, and their imagined future is often seen as a safety signal. We introduce SteerTrace, a targeted universal attack on WAMs that also traces its effect inside the model. One $\ell_\infty$-bounded (4/255) perturbation per model, shared across frames, tasks, and camera views, pushes the executed action toward an attacker-chosen target. On eight WAMs from four families (FastWAM, Faster-WAM, OpenWAM and LingBot-VA) on LIBERO and RoboTwin, it drives task success close to zero and moves the attacked action toward the target. Same-budget noise and BadWAM produce little action shift in the target direction. We use the signed targeted effect to measure how much of the effect each intervention removes or reproduces. Under the standard attack, restoring future tokens removes 61-94% of the targeted effect in the five models whose action experts attend to them. When the attack is constrained to keep the predicted future close to the clean prediction, the targeted effect can become more dependent on current-frame tokens. In three of these five models, restoring current-frame tokens removes 87-99% of the effect, compared with 9-15% for future tokens. In six models, restoring just 0.36% of the FFN neurons in layers 10-29 of the video expert removes 72-96% of the targeted effect. Residual directions 2-16 hold only 11-18% of the shift energy, yet removing them cancels most of the targeted effect.

</details>


### [81] [DACA: Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25722)
*Jiaming Liu,Linghe Kong,Guixu Zhang,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双锚协作智能体DACA，将3D视觉定位重构为语义-几何双锚协作闭环推理范式，零样本性能显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-VG智能体虽摆脱对预处理点云的依赖，但采用单向流水线式工具调用架构，将语义理解与几何重建割裂，导致误差累积且无法自主从失败中恢复。

Method: DACA包含两个互补专用工具：语义锚工具保存目标的视觉语义特征，借助光流与VLM局部验证实现高效目标跟踪；几何锚工具保存目标的三维空间几何特征，通过体素化与多关键点投影进行全局空间推理。两者通过标准化工具调用协议双向交换信息，自主决定下一步感知-动作，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在ScanRefer与Nr3D基准上，零样本DACA在ScanRefer上取得73.6%的整体Acc@0.25，在Nr3D上取得70.4%的整体准确率，远超最新零样本3D-VG方法。

Conclusion: 双锚协作的闭环推理范式有效缓解流水线架构的误差累积与不可恢复问题，为3D视觉定位提供了新的零样本解决思路。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3D-VG) is a task that bridges the natural language and 3D space, serving as a foundational capability for embodied intelligence and human-computer interaction. Although recent 3D-VG agents overcome the reliance on pre-processed 3D point clouds, they essentially employ a unidirectional, pipeline-style tool-calling architecture. This architecture separates semantic understanding from geometric reconstruction, leading to error accumulation and an inability to autonomously recover from failures. To address this flaw, this paper proposes the Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent (DACA), which reinterprets 3D-VG as a semantic-geometric dual-anchor collaborative reasoning paradigm. DACA comprises two complementary specialized tools: the semantic anchor tool holds the visual semantic features of the target and achieves efficient target tracking via optical flow and VLM local verification; the geometric anchor tool holds the 3D spatial geometric features of the target and performs global spatial reasoning via voxelization and multi-keypoint projection. The two components exchange information bidirectionally via a standardized tool call protocol, autonomously deciding on the next perception-action step to form a closed-loop optimization. Experiments on the ScanRefer and Nr3D benchmarks demonstrate that zero-shot DACA achieves an overall Acc@0.25 of 73.6\% on ScanRefer and 70.4\% overall accuracy on Nr3D, far surpassing the latest zero-shot 3D-VG methods. The code and models are available at https://github.com/liujia99/DACA

</details>


### [82] [Verifying Before Enhancing: Target Evidence Chain Verification for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25724)
*Jiayi Zuo,Songwei Pei,Shuhuai Wang,Yifei Wu,Shangguang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VBE-UNet（目标证据链验证网络），通过反事实背景解释与联合证据验证构建互补证据，超越单纯响应强度评估候选目标可靠性，实现红外小目标高精度、低虚警检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标尺寸小、纹理弱、边界模糊，易与复杂背景混淆；且类目标背景结构在多尺度上产生显著响应，仅凭响应强度无法可靠区分真实目标与背景干扰。

Method: 构建证据链验证框架：反事实背景解释模块对局部目标证据建模；联合证据验证整合跨尺度支持与基于扰动的贡献验证，指导特征重组；由可靠性估计驱动选择性特征精炼，增强目标相关响应、抑制无证据支撑的背景激活。

Result: 在IRSTD-1K、NUDT-SIRST、NUAA-SIRST数据集上，检测精度优于现有SOTA方法且虚警率更低；消融实验与证据可靠性分析验证了验证框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 以互补证据验证替代单一响应强度判据，可有效提升红外小目标检测的准确性与抗虚警能力。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is essential for early warning, surveillance, remote sensing, and space-based target perception. However, infrared small targets are typically tiny, weakly textured, and boundary-blurred, making them difficult to distinguish from complex backgrounds. Moreover, target-like background structures can produce salient responses across multiple scales, making response strength alone insufficient for reliable target discrimination. To address this challenge, we propose VBE-UNet, a Target Evidence Chain Verification Network that assesses candidate reliability beyond response magnitude through complementary evidence cues. Specifically, counterfactual background explanation models local target evidence, while joint evidence verification integrates cross-scale support and perturbation-based contribution verification to guide feature recomposition. The resulting reliability estimates guide selective feature refinement, promoting target-relevant responses while reducing unsupported background activations. Extensive experiments on IRSTD-1K, NUDT-SIRST, and NUAA-SIRST demonstrate that VBE-UNet achieves superior detection accuracy and lower false-alarm rates compared with state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies and evidence reliability analysis further validate the effectiveness of the proposed verification framework.

</details>


### [83] [What's in the box? Revealing Object Layouts Inside Opaque Boxes via Contactless Vibration Excitation and Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25728)
*Ethan Turok,Matan Kichler,Shai Bagon,Mark Sheinin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WhisperBox：用扬声器激励不透明箱体振动，以激光散斑网格非接触测量可见壁振动，学习编码-解码得到箱内物体的二维占据布局，并具备跨物体、位置、朝向、重量及激光/激励源变化的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 箱内物体改变其机械响应，为"隔箱感知"提供线索；现有方法多需接触式激励/传感，缺乏完全远程推断箱内物体二维空间布局的手段。

Method: 近场扬声器非接触激励箱体振动；在一面可见壁上布置激光点阵，用相机测量各点散斑图样位移以获取表面振动场；WhisperBox 模型将振动编码为隐表征并解码为箱内物体的占据图（occupancy map）。

Result: 在多种箱体、单/多物体场景中恢复布局；泛化到未见过的物体实例、位置、朝向与重量，以及激光布点与激励源的改变。分析表明：有信息量的频带不一定与箱体共振峰重合；隐空间编码了物体摆放信息并支持算术运算。

Conclusion: 远程振动感知可用于推断视野之外的空间布局，为"透过遮挡物"的非接触式内部状态估计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Can we reveal what is inside an opaque box by observing its surface vibrations? Objects inside a box alter its mechanical response, providing cues about their hidden locations. We introduce a method that uses these cues to predict a box's 2D occupancy layout entirely remotely. A nearby speaker excites vibrations, which we measure on one visible wall using a grid of laser points and a camera measuring each point's speckle-pattern shifts. Our model, WhisperBox, learns to encode these vibrations into a latent representation and decode it into an occupancy map of the box's contents. The method requires no physical contact to excite or sense vibrations. We demonstrate layout recovery across various boxes and single- and multiple-object scenes, with generalization to unseen object instances, positions, orientations, and weights, as well as changes in laser placement and excitation source. Our analysis reveals that informative frequency bands need not coincide with the box's resonances and that the learned latent space captures object placement and supports arithmetic operations. These results demonstrate the potential of remote vibration sensing to infer spatial layouts hidden from view.

</details>


### [84] [CGM: Chaos-Guided Graph Attention and Expert Routing for Autism Stereotypy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25730)
*Hafsa Aslam,Yue Li,Saba Aslam,Abdur Rasool,Qiao Pan,Muhammad Rizwan,Ovidiu Bagdasar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混沌引导的图注意力与专家混合模型（CGM），用逐关节逐帧的角速度样本熵张量驱动图注意力与专家路由，在孤独症刻板行为视频上达到0.930平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架图注意力仅按内容（运动幅度）加权关节，使抖动的手腕与静止关节权重相同；但刻板行为的定义在于运动的不可预测性而非运动量。在本队列中，所有幅度类运动学描述符在刻板片段内均失效，唯有窗口化角速度样本熵显著上升（Hedges' g=+1.05），说明需要以不规则性而非幅度作为建模信号。

Method: 将角速度样本熵计算为逐关节、逐帧的张量，并使其在结构上驱动计算：(1) 在图注意力logit上加入学习的成对项，把注意力集中到不规则关节并将剩余边权重重新排序至共同不规则的关节对；(2) 以同一信号为条件的门控将每帧路由到四个专家中的两个，专家权重跟踪帧级不规则性。采用按被试分组的五折交叉验证。

Result: 在116个片段上达到0.930平衡准确率，较图注意力基线提升+0.187；消融显示仅混沌调制贡献+0.132，而无不规则性的路由无可靠增益；成对项系数既不消失也不反转，将张量置零后94%片段注意力重新对齐；在公开MMASD上组件排序可复现；在未见过的Panoptic Studio会话上无需重训练即保持0.86特异度（无混沌基线至多0.35）；由同一张量得到的严重度指数分半信度0.87，区分队列Hedges' g=+1.08，且在儿童跨治疗录像内保持一致。

Conclusion: 以运动不规则性（混沌/熵）而非幅度作为结构性归纳偏置，可显著提升刻板行为识别的准确率、泛化性与可解释性，并支持干预过程中的纯视频刻板行为监测。

Abstract: Skeleton graph attention for autism video analysis weights joints by content alone, giving an erratic wrist the same standing as a still one. Stereotypy, however, is defined by how unpredictably movement unfolds rather than how much of it occurs: in our cohort, every amplitude-based kinematic descriptor falls in stereotyped clips, while windowed Sample Entropy of angular velocity alone rises (Hedges' g = +1.05). We compute this irregularity as a per-joint, per-frame tensor that structurally drives computation. The proposed chaos-guided graph attention and mixture-of-experts model (CGM) couples two mechanisms. First, a learned pairwise term added to the graph-attention logit concentrates attention on irregular joints and re-ranks the remaining edge weight toward jointly irregular pairs; its unconstrained coefficient neither vanishes nor inverts, and zeroing the tensor realigns attention in 94% of clips. Second, a gate conditioned on the same signal routes each frame to two of four experts, whose weights track frame-level irregularity. Under subject-grouped five-fold cross-validation on 116 clips, CGM reaches 0.930 balanced accuracy, exceeding the graph-attention baseline by +0.187; the component ordering replicates on the public MMASD dataset, and on held-out Panoptic Studio sessions, CGM keeps 0.86 specificity without retraining, against at most 0.35 for chaos-free baselines. In ablation, routing without irregularity yields no reliable gain, whereas chaos modulation alone contributes +0.132. A severity index from the same tensor reaches 0.87 split-half reliability, separates the cohorts with Hedges' g = +1.08, and stays consistent within children across therapy recordings, supporting video-only monitoring of stereotypy during intervention.

</details>


### [85] [Towards High-Fidelity Learned Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25731)
*Bumyoon Kim,Yongseong Kim,Inhyuk Jeong,Byeungwoo Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种具备搜索能力的学得帧间编解码器，通过在编码端搜索块运动、在大潜容量学得块变换中选取最优划分并联合优化率失真代价，将学得编解码器的质量覆盖范围从专业质量区间的24–38%提升至100%（或受限配置下的55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业视频工作流所需的画质超出当前学得视频编解码器所能达到的水平。作者量化了学得编解码器在专业质量区间（从VVC QP37到ProRes 4:2:2高档）的覆盖度，发现近期学得编解码器仅覆盖24–38%，原因是其编码器为前馈网络、潜在容量固定且极低（≤0.25），且量化去除后质量提升至多1 dB，说明瓶颈不在量化而在编码器的搜索能力与表示容量。

Method: 在10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2测试序列上，统一在所有帧内位置提供原始帧作为参考，以仅比较帧间编码。提出一种编码端执行搜索的学得帧间编解码器：搜索块运动并由学得网络精化；在潜在容量最高达1的多个学得块变换间搜索，依据率失真代价选择块划分、skip决策和量化系数。还设置将所有残差块固定为16x16（潜在容量0.25）的对照配置。

Result: 所提编解码器在全部六条序列上达到专业质量区间的上端（即100%覆盖）；当所有残差块固定为16x16、潜在容量为0.25时，同一编解码器覆盖区间的55%，且无论是否使用量化均如此，进一步表明这一档性能由搜索与结构而非量化决定。

Conclusion: 学得编解码器难以达到专业画质的主要原因在于编码器缺乏搜索机制与潜在表示容量受限，而非量化。引入传统式搜索（运动、变换划分、skip决策）与更大的学得变换容量，可使学得帧间编码在专业质量区间内达到甚至覆盖全范围。

Abstract: Professional video workflows operate at qualities that learned video codecs do not reach. We measure how much of a professional quality range a codec covers, from the lowest quality of the standard test conditions, VVC at QP 37, to the highest 4:2:2 grade of the production codec ProRes. To compare inter-frame coding alone, every codec receives original frames as references at the intra positions. On 10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2 test sequences, four recent learned codecs cover 24 to 38% of this range. Their encoders are feed-forward networks with a fixed latent capacity, the number of latent values per sample, of at most 0.25, and removing their quantization raises the quality by at most 1 dB. We propose a learned inter-frame codec whose encoder searches, as the encoders of conventional codecs do. It searches block motion, which a learned network refines, and it searches among learned block transforms whose latent capacity ranges up to 1, selecting the partition, the skip decisions, and the quantized coefficients by a rate-distortion cost. Our codec reaches the upper end of the range on all six sequences. With all residual blocks fixed to 16x16, whose latent capacity is 0.25, the same codec covers 55% of the range, with or without quantization.

</details>


### [86] [SAM 3 Sees More Than It Detects: Promptable Concept Maps Beyond Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25747)
*Yu-Huan Wu,Yun Liu,Liangli Zhen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 promptable concept maps (PCM) 任务与 PCM-Bench 基准，并给出 UNVEIL 方法，将 SAM 3 的语义路径扩展为可用短语提示生成概念图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示分割器（如 SAM 3）只回答"是什么"（名词实例），而密集预测任务还需回答类别无关问题：什么最突出或隐藏、光如何被遮挡/透射/反射、结构在哪里。作者发现 SAM 3 的语义路径其实对这些概念有响应，但低图像级存在性分数会阻断实例路径的全部检测。

Method: 构建 PCM-Bench，按所问问题归组七个标准测试集；对 SAM 3 语义路径的副本用 LoRA 微调其最后若干 block，并训练独立的存在性解码器，从经验证的负样本中学习概念是否出现；每个查询仅用一条文本短语。与 SAM 3 并行运行，不改变原输出。

Result: 在七个任务上，UNVEIL 在同类可提示模型中得分最高，超过 SAM 3 官方微调与基于多模态大模型的流水线；同一组权重既覆盖显著/伪装物体、阴影、透明物体、镜面等区域概念，也能映射边缘、骨架等细结构及物体类别。

Conclusion: 概念图可作为实例分割的类别无关补充；瓶颈在存在性判断而非语义感知，解耦存在性解码可释放 SAM 3 语义路径的潜力。

Abstract: Promptable segmenters answer what a thing is. Dense prediction tasks also ask class-agnostic questions, such as what stands out or hides, how light is blocked, transmitted or reflected, and where structure lies. Robots, surveillance systems and image editors build on their answers. We study promptable concept maps (PCM), which extend SAM 3's promptable concept segmentation from noun instances to maps of concepts named by short noun phrases such as `"transparent object", with "none" as an answer. PCM-Bench groups the standard test sets of seven such tasks by the question they ask. On it, we find that SAM 3 sees more than it detects. Its semantic path responds to many of these concepts, yet a low image-level presence score can block every detection of its instance path. We present UNVEIL, which trains a copy of SAM 3's semantic path for these maps, with LoRA on copies of its last blocks. A separate presence decoder learns from verified negatives whether a concept occurs. With one text phrase per query, UNVEIL maps region concepts such as salient and camouflaged objects, shadows, transparent objects and mirrors. The same weights map thin structures such as edges and skeletons as well as object categories. Run next to SAM 3, it leaves SAM 3's own outputs unchanged. On each of the seven tasks, UNVEIL reaches the highest score among the compared promptable models, including SAM 3's official fine-tuning and pipelines built on multimodal LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhuan-wu/UNVEIL.

</details>


### [87] [AdaptReg: Skip-level FiLM Adapter for Task-Specific Adaptation of a Frozen Registration Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25761)
*Shuyue Shi,Rong Yuan,Lin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaptReg：在冻结的配准基础模型uniGradICON上添加轻量可训练适配分支，通过FiLM式逐体素调制解码器跳跃连接，并用病灶体积保持损失监督，实现前列腺癌纵向PSMA-PET/CT配准中病灶体积一致、MTV/TLG测量可靠的形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向PSMA-PET/CT配准不仅要求一般对齐精度，更需保持病灶体积，使MTV与TLG的变化反映真实治疗反应而非配准伪影；通用配准模型难以满足此任务特定要求。

Method: 保持uniGradICON完全冻结，增设轻量适配分支：从冻结的TotalSegmentator编码器中提取解剖语义特征，生成FiLM式逐体素仿射调制参数(γ, β)注入uniGradICON解码器跳跃连接；采用恒等保持初始化，并以病灶体积保持损失监督训练。

Result: Codabench官方验证集(20对)：DSC 0.7585、HD95 6.33 mm、MTV/TLG误差1.68%/2.11%、非微分同胚体素0.0022%；较uniGradICON+实例优化基线DSC提升0.05，MTV/TLG误差降低70.9%/67.7%。隐藏测试集(160对)：CT DSC 0.816、PET DSC 0.598、MTV/TLG误差4.02%/3.46%，在PSMAReg评测16支队伍中排名第四。

Conclusion: 以参数高效适配方式改造冻结配准基础模型，可在保持形变微分同胚性的同时显著提升病灶体积保持能力与MTV/TLG测量可靠性，验证了任务特定适配范式的有效性。

Abstract: Longitudinal PSMA-PET/CT registration is a key step in assessing treatment response in prostate cancer, and it requires more than generic alignment accuracy: the deformation must preserve lesion volume, so that measured changes in metabolic tumor volume (MTV) and total lesion glycolysis (TLG) reflect treatment response rather than registration artifacts. We propose AdaptReg, which adapts a frozen registration foundation model to such task-specific requirements through a lightweight trainable adapter branch with identity-preserving initialization. Keeping uniGradICON fully frozen, we apply FiLM-style per-voxel affine modulation ($γ$, $β$) to its decoder skip connections; the modulation is generated from anatomical semantic features of a frozen TotalSegmentator encoder and supervised by a lesion volume-preservation loss. On the official Codabench validation set (20 pairs), AdaptReg achieves DSC 0.7585, HD95 6.33 mm, MTV/TLG errors of 1.68%/2.11%, and 0.0022% non-diffeomorphic voxels, improving on the uniGradICON + instance optimization baseline by $+0.05$ DSC and reducing the MTV/TLG errors by 70.9%/67.7%. On the hidden official test set (160 pairs), AdaptReg achieves CT DSC 0.816, PET DSC 0.598, and MTV/TLG errors of 4.02%/3.46%, ranking fourth among 16 teams in the final PSMAReg evaluation. Code: https://github.com/shishuyue/AdaptReg

</details>


### [88] [Driver Gaze Target Identification via Gaze Estimation and Structured Driving Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25771)
*Xinze Cao,Ke Wang,Deyong Guan,Yuanquan Li,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个模块化框架，从同步的车内驾驶员图像与前视道路图像中识别物体或区域级别的注视目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注视区域分类与连续注意力定位只能提供有限信息，无法确定被注视场景目标的身份，而物体级注视目标信息对智能车辆中的视觉注意力监测更具价值。

Method: 框架包含四个组件：MFGaze（MAE预训练的ViT结合FiLM头部姿态调制）；全景场景理解（构建结构化候选集）；多项式岭回归（经个体化标定将注视特征映射到2D场景图像坐标）；熵驱动的目标推断（自适应融合边界距离几何与类别级语义先验）。

Result: 实验室实验中整体Top-1注视目标识别准确率达96.27%；序列特定标定后，在LBW数据集上任务相关物体Top-1为79.92%，道路类别为85.58%；MFGaze在MPIIFaceGaze、ETH-XGaze、Gaze360上平均角度误差最低，分别为4.06°、4.23°、10.52°，并具强跨数据集泛化性；超参数扰动下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 结果支持该框架作为驾驶员监测中物体级注视信息的来源。

Abstract: Identifying a driver's gaze target provides object-level information for visual attention monitoring in intelligent vehicles. Gaze-zone classification and continuous attention localization provide limited information about the identity of the attended scene target. This paper proposes a modular framework that identifies object- or region-level gaze targets from synchronized in-cabin driver images and forward-view road images. The framework comprises four components: MFGaze, which combines a Vision Transformer pretrained with a masked autoencoder (MAE) and head-pose conditioning through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM); panoptic scene understanding, which constructs a structured candidate set; polynomial ridge regression, which maps gaze features to 2D scene-image coordinates through individualized calibration; and entropy-driven target inference, which adaptively fuses boundary-distance geometry with class-level semantic priors. Experiments evaluate the framework using three public gaze datasets, a laboratory test using Cityscapes road scenes and self-collected gaze data, and the Look Both Ways (LBW) dataset. The complete framework achieves an overall Top-1 gaze target identification accuracy of 96.27% in the laboratory experiment. After sequence-specific calibration, it achieves Top-1 accuracies of 79.92% for task-relevant objects and 85.58% for the road class on LBW. MFGaze achieves the lowest mean angular errors among the compared methods, with 4.06$^\circ$, 4.23$^\circ$, and 10.52$^\circ$ on MPIIFaceGaze, ETH-XGaze, and Gaze360, respectively, and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Sensitivity analyses show stable performance under hyperparameter perturbations. These results support the framework as a source of object-level gaze information for driver monitoring.

</details>


### [89] [Suppressing the Shortcut Hidden Behind the Gains of VideoLLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25776)
*Youngwoo Shin,Jongsuk Kim,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VETO方法：在微调更新中通过正交投影移除纯文本捷径子空间，使VideoLLM真正依赖视频输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型(VideoLLM)在SFT阶段会学到依赖训练数据中的文本规律来作答，形成绕过视频的"捷径"。该捷径在数据层面难以定义，且与训练轮次、数据规模和骨干网络无关；它隐藏在训练分布内准确率提升的背后，却并非提升所必需，反而使SFT在分布偏移下丧失视频带来的收益。

Method: VETO不干预数据，而是在捷径"生成"的微调更新处进行干预：标定基座模型激活中仅由文本占据的子空间，并将微调更新投影到其正交补空间，从而在捷径形成之初即抑制它，而无需显式定义捷径。简称 Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection，代码开源于 https://github.com/Youngwoo-git/VETO。

Result: 在七个VideoLLM上，VETO保持了SFT在训练分布上的增益并抑制了捷径；在六个分布偏移基准上，其收益超过SFT，且模型基于完整输入而非捷径作答。

Conclusion: 在参数更新空间而非数据层面消除文本捷径是可行且有效的，可使VideoLLM真正基于视频证据作答，并在分布偏移下获得更好泛化。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the stage in which Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) advance their capability to reason over video. However, the VideoLLM also learns to answer from textual regularities of its training data, a shortcut that bypasses the video. The regularities behind the shortcut are hard to define at the data level, and the shortcut forms regardless of training epoch, data size, or backbone. The shortcut is hidden behind a rise in accuracy within the training distribution, yet it is not required for the rise and even costs SFT the gain the video offers under distribution shift. We therefore propose Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection (VETO), which intervenes on the fine-tuning update where the shortcut is written rather than on the data. VETO calibrates the subspace that the text alone occupies in the activations of the base model and projects the update onto its orthogonal complement. The VideoLLM is thus left to ground its answer in the full input, with the shortcut suppressed as it forms and never defined. Across seven VideoLLMs, VETO preserves the gain of SFT on the training distribution and suppresses the shortcut. On six benchmarks under shift it gains more than SFT and answers from the full input rather than the shortcut. Code is available at https://github.com/Youngwoo-git/VETO.

</details>


### [90] [GeoPillars: A Large-Kernel Pillar Detector for Single-Sweep LiDAR Under Point Sparsity, and a Decomposition of Its Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25787)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Shubham Sarkar,Akash Mohapatra,Ghanasham Patil,Sheetal Girase*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPillars是一个在每0.4 m pillar至多8点的硬预算下工作的单帧LiDAR pillar式3D检测器，其相对CenterPoint的+13.76 BEV AP@0.7提升中约10.19点可归因于检测头分辨率提升，新组件贡献有限；真实nuScenes零样本迁移全部失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的pillar点数预算（每0.4 m pillar 8点，丢弃46.6%有效点）下，研究如何在不增加输入点数的前提下提升pillar式单帧LiDAR 3D检测精度，并通过逐级消融厘清性能提升的真实来源。

Method: 提出GeoPillars，整合几何pillar特征、pillar内分组向量注意力、BEV网格上的可学习绝对位置先验、大核主干、辅助表面法向监督与全分辨率检测头；在123k合成CARLA扫描上训练，并在7个模型间做逐级消融与朝向误差、推理速度对比，另在6,743帧真实nuScenes关键帧上做零样本评估。

Result: 同预算下81.33 BEV AP@0.7，较CenterPoint同预算+13.76、较其原生稠密预算+1.91；消融显示仅提升检测头分辨率即贡献10.19点，编码器/主干/neck/位置先验联合贡献3.37点，法向监督仅0.20点且在运行方差内；折叠朝向翻转后各模型朝向误差相近，GeoPillars朝向反转约为分辨率匹配基线的一半；七模型中速度最慢；nuScenes零样本无迁移，仅保留3.7%–12.2%精度；LiDAR强度采集缺陷使所有模型仅依赖几何。

Conclusion: 该工作表明所得增益主要来自被忽视的检测头分辨率而非新颖模块，且验证集按帧而非按场景划分导致结果为分布内指标；合成到真实的零样本泛化与效率仍是pillar式单帧检测的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: GeoPillars is a pillar-based 3D detector for single-sweep LiDAR under a hard budget of eight points per 0.4 m pillar, a budget that discards 46.6% of in-range points. It adds geometric pillar features, grouped vector attention within each pillar, a learned absolute positional prior on the bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid, a large-kernel backbone, auxiliary surface-normal supervision and a full-resolution detection head. Trained on 123k synthetic CARLA scans and evaluated on cars, it reaches 81.33 BEV AP@0.7, 13.76 points above CenterPoint at the same input budget and 1.91 points above CenterPoint at its native, denser budget. These are in-distribution figures, because the validation split is per-frame rather than scene-disjoint. A rung-by-rung decomposition shows that the new components explain little of the gain. Raising the detection-head resolution alone recovers 10.19 of the 13.76 points, the joint change of encoder, backbone, neck and positional prior adds 3.37, and the normal-supervision branch adds 0.20, which is within run-to-run variation. With heading reversals folded out, orientation error is similar for all seven models, although GeoPillars reverses headings about half as often as a resolution-matched baseline. It is also the slowest of the seven models. Evaluated zero-shot on 6,743 real nuScenes keyframes, no model transfers, and each retains 3.7% to 12.2% of its CARLA accuracy. A collection defect left LiDAR intensity constant, so every model learned from geometry alone. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

</details>


### [91] [Fast and Accurate Body Estimation in Pixels, World Space and Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25801)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAB 是一种兼顾推理效率与恢复精度的人体估计器，直接预测骨架关节与稠密表面点，实时运行并支持接触预测、世界空间运动估计与人到人形机器人的运动重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多仅回归参数化网格模型参数，推理效率与恢复保真度难以兼得；同时缺少将人体与场景（接触、深度）及人形机器人衔接的统一方案。

Method: FAB 直接预测人体骨架关节与稠密表面点，并预测稠密 2D/3D 身体点与参数化 MHR 头部，再以一次学习式拟合加两步 Gauss-Newton 拟合 SMPL；ViT-L 学生模型 FAB-L 由微调后的 SAM 3D Body 蒸馏得到。网络还预测脚跟、脚趾、手、膝的接触点，估计世界空间运动，融合可见人体周围静态场景深度并用预测接触对齐人体与场景；通过内置 G1 头与运动级重定向器 FAB-Retarget（读取稠密点或拟合网格）实现人到 Unitree G1 的实到仿迁移。

Result: FAB-L 在 RTX 4070 Ti SUPER 上对单人从检测到拟合实时运行，速度为 SAM 3D Body eager-PyTorch 纯人体路径的 4.6 倍，且在多数相机空间基准上精度更优；FAB-Retarget 生成的参考动作比 SOMA、GMR、UMR、OmniRetarget 等专用重定向器更贴合人体，并显著优于用序列 IK 重定向的 Fast SAM 3D Body。

Conclusion: FAB 以稠密点预测为核心，统一了高效人体估计、场景接触对齐与运动重定向，验证了精度与实时性的可兼得性及其实到仿迁移价值。

Abstract: We present FAB (Fast and Accurate Body estimation), a human body estimator that jointly improves inference efficiency and body-recovery fidelity. Rather than predicting only parametric mesh-model parameters, FAB directly predicts human skeleton joints and dense surface points. In images, it predicts dense 2D and 3D body points alongside a parametric Momentum Human Rig (MHR) head, then fits SMPL in one learned pass followed by two Gauss-Newton steps. Its ViT-L student, FAB-L, distilled from a fine-tuned SAM 3D Body, runs in real time for one person on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, from detection to a fitted body, at 4.6x the speed of SAM 3D Body's eager-PyTorch body-only path, while improving accuracy on most camera-space benchmarks. The same network predicts heel, toe, hand, and knee contacts, which connect the body to the scene. FAB also estimates world-space motion, fuses static-scene depth around the visible person, and aligns body and scene using predicted contacts. For human-to-humanoid real-to-sim transfer, an in-model G1 head and FAB-Retarget, a motion-level retargeter that reads dense points or the fitted mesh, map motion to a Unitree G1. FAB-Retarget produces references that follow the person more closely than specialized retargeters including SOMA, GMR, UMR, and OmniRetarget, and substantially more closely than Fast SAM 3D Body retargeted with our sequence IK. Project page: https://www.jinkuncao.com/FAB.

</details>


### [92] [World-delta: Continually Integrating Observations for Incremental 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25802)
*Haonan Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Xiaobin Hu,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出World-delta增量式3D场景生成框架，通过不确定性感知的持久世界建模与delta引导扩散，实现对3D世界的持续扩展与纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法针对固定输入合成场景，难以随新观测持续扩展与修正；迭代式方法将已生成内容视为既定结构而非可修正预测，导致早期生成误差在新证据下仍持续累积。

Method: 框架含三部分：1) 视觉证据编码，将输入图像的几何与语义线索整合为统一world tokens；2) 观测校准世界建模，维护持久潜在世界状态，区分观测支持内容与临时补全假设，新证据触发选择性world delta以修正冲突假设并保留可靠内容；3) 增量世界扩散生成，用delta导出的掩码将扩散限制在需更新区域，冻结未变区域，仅解码并整合受影响3D块。

Result: 实验表明在增量3D场景生成、观测驱动的修正、场景一致性及连续更新下的更新效率方面均有效。

Conclusion: 通过区分观测证据与生成假设并以delta驱动局部更新，World-delta可在保持场景一致性的同时实现持续的3D世界扩展与错误修正，并减少冗余生成。

Abstract: Existing 3D generation methods synthesize visually compelling scenes from fixed inputs, yet struggle to continually expand and revise a 3D world as new observations arrive. Iterative approaches often fuse real observations and generated content into a single representation, treating generated content as established structure rather than revisable predictions. Consequently, early generation errors can persist and accumulate despite new evidence. We propose World-delta, an incremental 3D scene generation framework combining uncertainty-aware persistent world modeling with delta-guided diffusion for continual scene expansion and correction. Our framework comprises three components: 1) Visual Evidence Encoding integrates geometric and semantic cues from incoming images into unified world tokens. 2) Observation-Calibrated World Modeling maintains a persistent latent world state that distinguishes observation-supported content from provisional completion hypotheses. New evidence drives selective world deltas to revise conflicting hypotheses while preserving well-supported content. 3) Incremental World Diffusion Generation uses delta-derived masks to restrict diffusion to regions requiring updates, freezing unchanged regions. Only affected 3D chunks are decoded and integrated into the existing scene. Together, these components enable continual world expansion and revision while reducing redundant generation and preserving scene consistency. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of World-delta in incremental 3D scene generation, observation-driven correction, scene consistency, and update efficiency over successive scene updates.

</details>


### [93] [What Does Anti-Hallucination Training Buy? Saying Less, Cheap Pruning, and the Rest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25814)
*Lingkai Bu,Qian Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对"偏好训练降低物体幻觉"这一结论做归因拆解：将各方法相对基座的增益分为"少说话"部分与超出简短性部分，并用一个刻意弱化的11特征事后探针复现后者，发现大部分增益可被该探针再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内每个新方法都以更低的CHAIR分数为卖点，但"分数下降"究竟由什么构成并不清楚：可能来自描述变短、物体召回降低，而非真正的幻觉抑制能力提升。作者希望在预注册、冻结判据下量化这部分增益的可归因成分。

Method: 1）召回对齐基线：把基座模型caption截断到与训练后模型相同的物体召回，剔除"说得少"带来的增益；2）剩余增益交由一个冻结的、仅11个特征的弱事后探针（在500张标注图上拟合）去复现；3）在LLaVA-1.5-7B上评估8个公开方法、COCO/AMBER/Object HalBench三个基准，并扩展至Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL2.5与SENTINEL；4）用同一度量给训练信号定价（探针标签、CLIP物体级标签 vs 真值标签DPO）；5）辅以人评检验探针删除句与截断删除句的错误率，并核查CHAIR评测器精度。

Result: 超出简短性的增益中，探针可复现至少2/3：COCO 7/8、AMBER 8/8、Object HalBench 6/8，RLAIF-V为唯一稳定例外；SENTINEL在两个骨干上均达标。自训DPO在Qwen2.5-VL成立、InternVL2.5上COCO失败（4种训练设计中的2种）。召回匹配下，基座+探针优于截断：COCO 8/9、AMBER 9/9，在两种新骨干上亦然，且以真实句子删除实现时同样成立。训练信号定价：探针标签与CLIP标签DPO分别恢复真值标签DPO超出简短性增益的0.70与0.87（Qwen2.5-VL），两者均优于等长随机标签，COCO上CLIP标签优于探针标签。人评显示探针删除的句子错误率高于截断会删除的句子；CHAIR评测器的人评精度低于预设门槛，作为局限报告。

Conclusion: 偏好训练所报告的幻觉下降，在扣除简短性效应后，很大一部分可被一个极弱的事后特征探针重现，说明这部分"改进"更接近可预测的输出取舍而非新学到的细粒度对齐能力；该结论在多数方法与骨干上成立但非普适（RLAIF-V、InternVL2.5-COCO为例外），同时需正视CHAIR评测器本身的精度局限。

Abstract: Preference training is the standard recipe for reducing object hallucination in vision-language models, and each new method reports a lower CHAIR score. We ask what that reduction consists of. Every trained system is compared with its base model's caption truncated to the same object recall, which removes the part of the gain that comes from saying less; the remainder is compared with what a deliberately weak, frozen, eleven-feature post-hoc probe fitted on 500 annotated images can reproduce. All criteria were specified in the analysis code and frozen before the outputs they judge existed. For eight public methods on LLaVA-1.5-7B, the probe reproduces at least two thirds of what training removes beyond saying less for 7 of 8 methods on COCO, 8 of 8 on AMBER and 6 of 8 on Object HalBench, with RLAIF-V the consistent exception; a later public method released for both LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL (SENTINEL) meets the same bar on both. On our own DPO checkpoints the accounting holds on Qwen2.5-VL but fails on InternVL2.5 on COCO (2 of 4 training designs). At matched recall, the base model plus the probe beats truncation for 8 of 9 LLaVA-1.5 systems on COCO and 9 of 9 on AMBER, on the two newer backbones, and when realized as actual sentence deletion. The same measure prices training signals: DPO on the model's own samples labelled by the probe or by an object-level CLIP score recovers 0.70 and 0.87 of the beyond-brevity gain of ground-truth-labelled DPO on Qwen2.5-VL (the probe 0.73 on InternVL2.5), both beat length-matched random labels, and CLIP labels buy more than probe labels on COCO. Human judgements show that the sentences the probe deletes are wrong more often than those truncation would delete; a human check of the CHAIR evaluator's precision falls below our pre-set bar, which we report as a limitation.

</details>


### [94] [What Places an Action Boundary? Structure-Aware Attribution for Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25815)
*Ruoshan Lan,Zongru Shao,Chieh Tsai,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知的时序证据归因框架，用于解释TAL检测器的边界定位依据，并在THUMOS14与EPIC-KITCHENS-100上验证其忠实性优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAL检测器需同时识别动作并回归时序边界，而定位仍是主要误差来源。理解"边界为何落在某处"需要对时序证据做忠实归因，但现有归因方法未考虑TAL的时序结构，也未针对边界定位任务进行定制。

Method: 基于Shapley-Shubik原理，在随机证据恢复顺序上平均贡献；利用检测器感受野限制被单独排序的时间步、并用软梯度路径近似每个排列以降低计算复杂度；针对边界预测引入单元结构恢复顺序与边界锚定参照（选择性移除邻域证据）；同时改造忠实性评测以刻画连续时序证据的联合效应与边界位移方向。

Result: 在THUMOS14和EPIC-KITCHENS-100上，配合TriDet与ActionFormer，归因忠实性均优于现有方法；归因分析与受控干预显示类分数变化不能可靠反映边界位移，表明分类所依赖的内容对边界定位信息不足。

Conclusion: 结构感知归因能有效揭示TAL检测器的边界定位依据；分类与定位的证据基础存在解耦，输入层面证据支持"分类依赖对边界位置信息量较少的内容"这一判断。

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) detectors must identify actions and estimate their temporal boundaries, but localization remains a major source of detection errors. While understanding what determines boundary placement requires faithful attribution of temporal evidence, existing attribution methods have not sufficiently addressed the temporal structure of TAL or the need to tailor attribution to boundary localization. We introduce a structure-aware attribution framework that averages contributions across random evidence-restoration orders following the Shapley-Shubik principle. We make it tractable by using the detector's receptive field to limit which time steps are ordered individually, and by approximating each order with a soft gradient path. For boundary predictions, we further introduce cell-structured restoration orders and boundary-anchored references that selectively remove adjacent evidence. We also adapt faithfulness evaluation to assess the joint effects of contiguous temporal evidence and the direction of boundary displacement. Experiments on THUMOS14 and EPIC-KITCHENS-100 with TriDet and ActionFormer demonstrate improved attribution faithfulness over existing methods. Attribution analysis and controlled interventions further reveal that class-score changes do not reliably track boundary displacement, providing input-level evidence that classification relies on content less informative about boundary placement. Code and evaluation artifacts will be publicly released.

</details>


### [95] [SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819)
*Nico Leuze,Samed Dogan,Maximilian Hoh,Henry Schaub,Alfred Schöttl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCCV-6D用全稀疏多视RGB-D单遍流程、规范坐标投票与注意力门控蒸馏，在IPD上以0.826 AP/1.13 s/场景、MV-YCB上96.1 ADD-S实现快速且具竞争力的工业6D位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业抓取（bin picking）场景存在杂乱、部分遮挡以及工件反光、无纹理等问题，要求位姿估计兼具高精度与低资源开销；现有多阶段6D位姿估计流程精度高但内存与耗时难以满足实际部署，而整体式单遍方法依赖无差别下采样或降低分辨率来控制成本。

Method: 提出SCCV-6D，一种全稀疏多视RGB-D位姿估计器：以毫米级精度重建三维空间；用分阶段热力图机制剔除背景；在最高分辨率上同时预测任意数量物体的类别、平移偏移与旋转；旋转采用规范坐标投票（canonical coordinate voting），每个点带置信度为其规范物体坐标投票，配合对称感知加权Procrustes求解实现闭式位姿恢复；由偏移投票引导簇内点恢复，省去耗时后处理；并复用热力图任务监督的注意力先验，提出注意力门控蒸馏目标对师生特征对齐进行重加权。

Result: 在IPD测试集上取得0.826 AP，每场景1.13 s，显著快于领先方法；在MV-YCB上达到96.1 ADD-S；在ROBI密集料箱场景中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 全稀疏单遍多视RGB-D框架可在保持竞争力的精度的同时大幅降低运行时间，无需复杂后处理，适用于工业抓取的实际部署约束。

Abstract: Industrial bin picking requires pose estimates that are accurate under clutter, partial occlusion, and the reflective, texture-less nature of manufactured parts. Most recent approaches to 6D pose estimation follow multi-stage pipelines. These achieve high accuracy, but incur memory and runtime footprints that do not cope with the operational constraints of many real-world industrial deployments. Holistic pipelines estimate poses in a single pass, with cost controlled through indiscriminate subsampling or lowered resolution. Continuing this line, we present SCCV-6D, a fully sparse multi-view RGB-D estimator. We reconstruct the 3D space at millimeter precision, prune background via a staged heatmap mechanism, and predict class, translation offset and rotation at highest resolution for an arbitrary number of objects simultaneously. Rotation is formulated as canonical coordinate voting. Points vote for their canonical object coordinates with an associated confidence, enabling closed-form pose recovery with a symmetry-aware weighted Procrustes solve. Along an in-cluster point recovery induced by offset votes, this removes time-consuming post-processing stages. We reuse the task-supervised attention priors from the heatmap formulation for a novel attention-gated distillation objective that reweights teacher-student feature alignment. On the IPD test split we report competitive 0.826 AP at 1.13 s per scene, significantly faster than the leading entries. On MV-YCB our approach reaches 96.1 ADD-S, and remains competitive even on the densely packed bins of ROBI.

</details>


### [96] [Limits of local curvature for constructing search subspaces in segmentation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25822)
*Maxim Bolshim,Alexander Kugaevskikh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验由局部曲率构造的子空间对语义分割模型有限步梯度更新场变化的表征能力，发现新曲率方向能显著改善局部响应表征，但几乎不改善梯度场变化表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作常用局部曲率（如泛化Gauss-Newton）方向构造低维搜索子空间，但缺乏对"有限步后梯度更新场变化"这一实际量的定量评估，因此需要考察曲率子空间到底表征了什么。

Method: 以曲率算子作用于实际输入位移所得的局部响应为核心量；在50张图像、两个分割模型上计算固定子空间损失增量与全空间损失增量之比随搜索半径的变化；在同10张图像上对两种模型比较初始梯度的一维子空间与两个秩16子空间（在相同初始状态、相同实际步长与向量下评估）；并做固定像素权重的分解，量化有限步余项。

Result: 损失增量比值随搜索半径增大而下降。将泛化Gauss-Newton方向加入初始梯度，使场变化表征的平均相对误差从0.922降至0.874（模型一），从0.819降至0.763（模型二）。同秩下加入初始梯度的响应方向几乎消除局部响应表征误差，但场变化平均误差不下降。分解显示有限步余项范数与场变化相当，且对步后场的平方投影误差对角贡献最大。

Conclusion: 评估搜索子空间应同时衡量其对梯度场变化的表征精度和对局部响应的表征精度；曲率方向主要捕获局部响应而非有限步场变化。

Abstract: We examine how well subspaces constructed from local curvature represent the change in the gradient update field after a finite step in semantic segmentation models. The local response is the action of the curvature operator on the actual input displacement. In a study of 50 images and two models, we measure the ratio of the loss increase attained in a fixed subspace to that attained in the full space. This ratio decreases as the search radius increases. On ten images from the same sample, we compare the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the initial gradient with two rank-16 subspaces for both models. The two equal-rank subspaces are assessed at the same initial states, using the same actual steps and measured vectors. Adding generalised Gauss-Newton directions to the initial gradient reduces the mean relative error in representing the field change from 0.922 to 0.874 for the first model and from 0.819 to 0.763 for the second. At the same rank, including the response direction for the initial gradient nearly eliminates the error in representing the local response. The mean error in representing the field change does not decrease. A decomposition with fixed pixel weights shows that the finite-step remainder is comparable in norm to the field change and makes the largest diagonal contribution to the squared projection error of the field after the step. Evaluation of a search subspace should measure how accurately it represents the field change and the local response.

</details>


### [97] [Fusing Multimodal CNN Features with Structurally-Masked Transformers for Hierarchical Sub-Pixel Tree Species Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25824)
*Mattia Ferrari,Lorenzo Bruzzone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次感知的多模态深度学习框架，融合PRISMA高光谱、高分辨率全色与DEM数据，用结构掩码Transformer强制森林大类与细粒度树种间的分类学一致性，实现亚像元森林组成解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载高光谱森林树种精细制图面临光谱高度相似与自然林分固有的亚像元异质性难题；传统CNN依赖空间上下文，在破碎化或混交林中易引入噪声。

Method: 多分支编码器：EfficientNetV2-based 1D-CNN处理光谱特征，孪生2D-CNN分别处理空间与地形特征，并耦合新型结构掩码Transformer；以二元转移张量调制Transformer槽嵌入并约束推理，实现宽泛森林类别与细粒度树种分布间的分类学一致性；在意大利阿尔卑斯、亚平宁中部与南部三个生物地理区的实地样地验证。

Result: 持续优于现有多标签与排序类SOTA范式，尤其在识别从属树种及其相对优势度方面；双向学习使亚像元组成特征反哺宏观森林类型预测，地形先验有效消解复杂地形下的光谱歧义。

Conclusion: 该知识驱动方法在所评估的PRISMA场景下支持区域尺度生物多样性监测与森林表征。

Abstract: Precise forest species mapping using spaceborne hyperspectral data is challenging due to high spectral similarity and the inherent sub-pixel heterogeneity of natural forest stands. While traditional deep learning models exploit spatial context through convolutional architectures, these approaches can introduce noise in fragmented or mixed-species forests. In this paper, we propose a hierarchy-aware, multimodal deep learning framework to resolve sub-pixel forest composition by integrating PRISMA (PRecursore IperSpettrale della Missione Applicativa) hyperspectral imagery, high-resolution panchromatic data, and digital elevation models. The architecture features a multi-branch encoder combining an EfficientNetV2-based one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) for spectral signatures and twin two-dimensional CNNs for spatial and topographic features, coupled with a novel structurally-masked Transformer. By using a binary transition tensor to condition the Transformer slot embeddings and constrain inference, the model enforces taxonomic consistency between broad forest categories and fine-grained species distributions. The framework is validated across three diverse Italian biogeographical regions (the Alps and the Central and Southern Apennines) using field-surveyed plots. The proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-label and ranking-based paradigms, particularly in identifying subordinate species and their relative dominance. The bidirectional learning mechanism allows sub-pixel compositional features to refine macroscopic forest type predictions, while the integration of topographic priors effectively resolves spectral ambiguities in complex terrains. This knowledge-driven method supports regional-scale biodiversity monitoring and forest characterization within the evaluated PRISMA scenarios.

</details>


### [98] [Rank2Replace: VLM-Ranked Slot Replacement for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25834)
*Bo-Han Feng,Keng-Te Liao,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rank2Replace：利用预训练视觉-语言模型按任务相关性对对象中心槽排序，并用另一图像同级槽嵌入进行替换式增强，提升小模型在分布偏移下的OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像预测器易受虚假相关影响：分布内精度高但分布偏移下泛化差。已有OOD方法常依赖环境标签或数据集特定调参，成本高且难以通用。

Method: 从预训练VLM迁移任务相关证据的知识：VLM对对象中心槽（object-centric slots）按任务相关性排序，指导与另一图像中同等级槽嵌入的rank-dependent替换。增强保留原图标签，促使模型依赖保留的任务证据而非偶然线索。可与现有OOD方法组合，VLM仅在训练期使用，推理时无需引导。

Result: 四个数据集、六个划分、共享冻结槽嵌入下，默认交叉熵配置在无环境标签、无数据集特定替换调参情况下取得宏平均平均组精度80.43%、最差组精度65.28%；槽腐蚀（slot corruption）对全部14种OOD训练方法的两项宏平均指标均有提升；与槽排序和IRM结合达81.96%与72.68%。消融验证排序与替换设计，增益跨5个排序VLM与6种提取器-预测器组合成立；置换不变预测器进一步提升两项指标。

Conclusion: 基于VLM任务相关性排序的槽替换增强是一种通用、无需环境标签、无需数据集特定调参的OOD泛化手段，可即插即用地提升多种OOD训练方法，且推理高效。

Abstract: Spurious correlations can give image predictors high in-distribution accuracy but poor generalization under distribution shift. We introduce $\textbf{Rank2Replace}$, which transfers knowledge of task-relevant evidence from pretrained vision--language models (VLMs) to small predictors through training-time augmentation for out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. The VLM ranks object-centric slots by task relevance and guides rank-dependent replacement with same-rank slot embeddings from another image. This augmentation retains the original image's label, encouraging reliance on preserved task evidence as incidental cues change, and supports combination with existing OOD methods. Across four datasets and six splits under shared frozen slot embeddings, the default cross-entropy configuration reaches macro-averaged average-group and worst-group accuracies of $80.43\%$ and $65.28\%$ without environment labels or dataset-specific replacement tuning. Slot corruption improves both macro-averaged metrics for all fourteen OOD training methods. Combining it with slot ranking and IRM reaches $81.96\%$ and $72.68\%$. Controlled ablations support the ranking and replacement design, with gains across five ranking VLMs and six extractor--predictor combinations. Permutation-invariant predictors further improve both macro-averaged metrics over their unaugmented counterparts while using VLM guidance only during training for efficient inference.

</details>


### [99] [ContextRenderer: Dynamic-Context Video Generation for Interactive Game Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25838)
*Runze Wang,Fei Zha,Bing He,Ke Xu,Zhongyuan Wu,Yuxuan Xie,Wenzheng Wu,Zhongyuan Liu,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContextRenderer，一个用于交互式游戏世界模型的自回归视频扩散渲染框架，通过动态上下文学习、循环历史压缩与跨表示流对齐，实现动态条件跟随与跨代理泛化，并构建 WorldPrism 数据集支撑训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式游戏世界模型将可执行的世界演化与视觉生成解耦，由视频模型将引擎提供的场景表示渲染为细节观测。但现有生成式渲染器在持续游玩过程中难以在跟随不断变化的条件的同时，保持跨多种代理表示与目标风格的视觉连续性。

Method: ContextRenderer 包含三个核心组件：(1) Dynamic-Context Learning (DCL)，在 chunk 级别提供交互监督；(2) Recurrent History Compression (RHC)，在有限 token 预算内压缩历史以跟随条件更新并保持视觉连续性；(3) Cross-Representation Flow Alignment (CRFA)，通过共享的高保真重建，将代理重建与外观、交互变化对齐。同时构建 WorldPrism 数据集，来源包括智能体构建世界、AAA 游戏与开源视频，并提供对应渲染结果与 chunk 级标注。

Result: 在 GWM-Bench 与 Solar-Bench 上，重建质量与视觉质量均有提升；定性结果展示了在提示与主体参考不断变化下长达数分钟的交互式渲染能力。

Conclusion: ContextRenderer 通过 DCL、RHC 与 CRFA 的协同设计，兼顾动态条件跟随、视觉连续性与跨代理泛化，配合 WorldPrism 数据集，验证了长时序交互式生成渲染的可行性。

Abstract: Interactive game world models separate executable world evolution from visual generation, using video models to render engine-provided scene representations into detailed observations. During sustained gameplay, however, existing generative renderers struggle to follow changing conditions while preserving visual continuity across diverse proxies and target styles. We therefore introduce ContextRenderer, an autoregressive video diffusion framework designed for both dynamic conditioning and cross-proxy generalization. It couples Dynamic-Context Learning (DCL) for chunk-level interaction supervision with Recurrent History Compression (RHC) to follow condition updates while preserving visual continuity within a bounded token budget. Across proxy representations, Cross-Representation Flow Alignment (CRFA) connects proxy reconstruction with appearance and interaction variation through a shared high-fidelity reconstruction. WorldPrism, our interaction-centric dataset constructed from agent-built worlds, AAA gameplay, and open-source videos, supplies the corresponding renderings and chunk-level annotations for these learning tasks. Experiments on GWM-Bench and Solar-Bench show improved reconstruction and visual quality, while qualitative results demonstrate minute-long interactive rendering under changing prompts and subject references. Project page: https://rainzor.github.io/contextrenderer/

</details>


### [100] [Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25840)
*Qiming Huang,Hanze Dong,Jipeng Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xinxing Xu,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mask-Evidence MedVQA 基准与 TUNE 证据管理框架，指出"获取正确的 mask"不足以支撑医疗视觉语言智能体的可靠推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗 VQA 数据集无法同时满足三个条件：将"证据使用"与"分割质量"解耦、覆盖多种医学图像类型、并能以多种渲染方式呈现同一 mask。因此难以回答"智能体是否有效利用了分割工具提供的证据"这一问题。

Method: 构建 Mask-Evidence MedVQA 基准（2.5K 样本、四类图像，每题配答案相关 GT mask 及多种渲染）；基于该基准开展证据呈现与证据权衡的实证分析；提出 TUNE 证据管理框架，显式管理"获取何种证据、如何呈现证据、是否接受答案更新"。

Result: 同一固定 mask 的不同渲染显著改变模型行为；引入 GT mask 可纠正部分错误答案，但同时会把部分原本正确的答案变为错误；证据管理使微调 Qwen3.5-9B 主干平均准确率提升 4.0 个百分点；完整 TUNE 在七个基准上平均超越直接工具使用 9.1 个百分点，并与现有医疗 VLM 与智能体方法具竞争力。

Conclusion: "拿到正确的 mask"并非终点：智能体还需决策证据的呈现方式以及是否据此修改答案；显式证据管理是可验证有效的改进方向。

Abstract: Medical vision-language agents increasingly rely on segmentation tools, and recent work has focused on acquiring the right mask, often treating a good mask as the end point of tool use. But do these agents use the resulting evidence effectively? To answer this question, we need a benchmark that separates evidence use from segmentation quality, spans multiple medical image types, and allows the same mask to be presented in different ways. Existing medical VQA datasets do not jointly provide these properties. We therefore construct Mask-Evidence MedVQA, a 2.5K-example benchmark spanning four image types, where each question is paired with an answer-relevant ground-truth mask and multiple possible renderings. Our study reveals two limitations of direct tool use. First, evidence presentation matters. Different renderings of the same fixed mask can substantially change model behavior. Second, masks introduce an evidence trade-off. Highlighting a relevant region can expose diagnostic evidence the model previously missed, but the added visual cue can also induce incorrect associations. As a result, even a ground-truth mask can correct some answers while turning initially correct ones into incorrect ones. Together, these findings show that acquiring the right mask is not enough. An agent must also decide how to present the evidence and whether it should change the answer. We therefore introduce TUNE (Tool Use is Not Enough), an evidence-management harness that explicitly manages what evidence to acquire, how to present it, and whether to accept the resulting answer update. Across three public medical VQA benchmarks, evidence management improves the fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B backbone's mean accuracy by 4.0 percentage points. Full TUNE improves over direct tool use by 9.1 points on average across seven benchmarks and achieves competitive results against existing medical VLMs and agents.

</details>


### [101] [TrajCal: Trajectory-Calibrated Time Schedules for Few-Step Flow Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25847)
*Junchi Zhang,Zihan Gu,Jiangcan Jia,Lulu Zhao,Wenbo Su,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架TrajCal，用scatter search元启发式为少步采样搜索固定时间步调度，提升与多步参考的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成质量高，但迭代采样需反复调用大型网络，成本高；现有少步方法易造成输出失真、与多步参考结果的一致性下降。

Method: 免训练的少步采样校准框架。以scatter search（元启发式）为核心：从多样化的种子调度出发，对候选方案进行变形（deform）与组合（combine），按最终输出保真度择优，选出一个固定调度复用于新输入；种子可为随机生成或由轨迹构造。

Result: 在图像与视频生成任务中，低评估预算下参考保真度优于所有对比基线，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量；随机种子即有效，轨迹型种子可进一步提升保真度；摄影图像上校准的调度在未见图像风格上也改善保真度，体现跨风格泛化。

Conclusion: 通过元启发式搜索固定采样调度，可在不训练、低预算条件下显著提升少步采样的参考保真度，并具备跨任务与跨风格可复用性。

Abstract: Flow matching enables high-quality image and video generation, but its iterative sampling process requires repeated evaluations of large neural networks. Existing few-step methods can distort the output and reduce its agreement with a many-step reference. We propose TrajCal, a training-free calibration framework for few-step sampling. Based on scatter search, a well-established metaheuristic for difficult optimization problems, TrajCal starts with diverse seed schedules, deforms and combines these candidates, and selects one fixed schedule by final output fidelity for reuse on new inputs. For both image and video generation, TrajCal achieves higher reference fidelity than all compared baselines at low evaluation budgets while maintaining competitive visual quality. The search is effective even with randomly generated seeds, while trajectory-based seeds further improve fidelity. For cross-style generalization, photography-calibrated schedules also improve fidelity on unseen image styles.

</details>


### [102] [EgoWitness: Benchmarking Instance-Level Spatial Memory and Reasoning in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25849)
*Zhengru Fang,Zehao Liu,Mingyu Tsoi,Yaoyu Zhu,Junlong Li,Fengmao Lv,Sijie Cheng,Yuanliang Sun,Fang Yang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EgoWitness 基准（8,453 道人工校验问题、约 324 小时周尺度与短时程自我中心视频、百万级掩码与房间时间线），用于测试多模态模型的实例级空间记忆与推理；并提出空间记忆智能体 EgoClue，通过五工具检索与两阶段 SFT+GRPO 训练，将 4B 控制器从 23.64% 提升至 72.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频基准多聚焦事件、物体类别或相对位置，缺乏对"何时何地最后一次见到某物体、谁看到"这类实例级空间记忆的系统评测，该能力在多模态模型中基本未被检验。

Method: 构建 EgoWitness 基准，覆盖七大能力（识别物体/房间、回忆见证者、房间访问推理等）；对 34 个注册地标与 21 类可移动物体标注超百万经审核掩码，并以房间时间线关联。提出 EgoClue 智能体：检索候选区间、验证实例身份、用五个工具计算时空关系，采用 SFT + GRPO 两阶段训练。

Result: 十二个直接 VLM 中开放权重模型最高仅 46.97%，在识别"谁最后看到物体"上无一超过 35%；EgoClue 在 1,320 道留出问题上将 4B 控制器从 23.64% 提升至 72.65%；证据轨迹合成在周尺度问题上达 74.85%（与最佳直接 VLM 持平），在训练外环境中达 80.56%。

Conclusion: 揭示现有 VLM 在实例级空间记忆上的显著不足，证明"工具化智能体 + 强化学习"范式在此任务上的有效性，并提供可复现的评测与训练资源。

Abstract: Recalling where and when an object was last seen and who saw it requires recognizing it across days and people. Existing long-video benchmarks focus on events, object categories, or relative locations, leaving instance-level spatial memory largely untested in multimodal models. To address this gap, we present EgoWitness, a benchmark for instance-level spatial memory and reasoning in egocentric video. Its 8,453 human-verified questions span approximately 324 hours of week-long and short-horizon recordings and test seven abilities, from recognizing an object or room to recalling who saw it and reasoning about room visits. Over one million reviewed masks of 34 registered landmarks and 21 movable-object labels are linked through room timelines. Among twelve direct vision-language models (VLMs), open-weight models reach at most 46.97% accuracy, and none exceeds 35% on identifying who saw an object last. We therefore propose EgoClue, a spatial-memory agent that retrieves candidate intervals, verifies instance identity, and computes spatial and temporal relations with five tools. Two-stage training with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) raises its 4B controller from 23.64% to 72.65% accuracy on 1,320 held-out questions. Evidence-trace synthesis matches the best direct VLM at 74.85% on week-long questions and achieves 80.56% in environments excluded from training.

</details>


### [103] [Thinking in Streams: Proactive Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25865)
*Junyoung Hong,Minsu Kim,Jaehyun Kim,Jeonghwan Cho,Jiwoo Lee,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“主动式空间推理”任务与ProSpace-Bench基准，并设计ProSpace-VLM，将因果估计的显式几何编码注入VLM中间层，以KV cache充当空间记忆并做空间token选择，实现流式视频中的及时响应与正确弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频中，判断空间条件是否成立需要随视角变化持续维护与更新空间知识；现有VLM缺乏在流式输入下“何时应答、是否应答”的主动空间推理能力。

Method: 构建ProSpace-Bench（涵盖物体、相机、场景三类共九项任务）与ProSpace-Train监督训练数据；提出ProSpace-VLM，将基于 incoming frames 因果估计的显式几何编码注入VLM中间层，复用KV cache作为空间记忆，并采用空间token选择以支持高效流式处理。

Result: 实验显示VLM与人类存在显著差距；ProSpace-VLM在及时响应与正确弃答两项指标上优于微调基线；分析刻画了模型在不同视角变化下对已观测空间信息的表征方式。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码与KV-cache空间记忆的结合可提升流式场景下的主动空间推理表现，但模型与人类水平仍有明显差距，跨视角空间知识的表征与更新仍是关键挑战。

Abstract: Recognizing when a requested spatial condition holds in streaming video can require maintaining and updating spatial knowledge across changing viewpoints. We study this capability through proactive spatial reasoning, where a model receives a query in advance and determines whether and when to respond as the stream unfolds. We introduce ProSpace-Bench, comprising nine tasks across object, camera, and scene reasoning, and ProSpace-Train for supervised training. To support spatial decisions, we propose ProSpace-VLM, which injects explicit geometric encodings estimated causally from incoming frames into intermediate VLM layers, using the existing KV cache as spatial memory and spatial token selection for efficient streaming. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between VLMs and humans, while ProSpace-VLM improves timely responses and correct abstention over fine-tuned baselines. Analyses examine how ProSpace-VLM represents previously observed spatial information across changing viewpoints.

</details>


### [104] [WHU-Road3D: A dataset and benchmark towards fine-grained road surface mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25873)
*Xiaoxin Mi,Zhen Cao,Chong Liu,Yuhao Li,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang,Yang Jia,Shengwu Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 WHU-Road3D 大规模多模态路面要素制图数据集，含约100公里中国公路与城市道路的MLS点云、全景影像、轨迹及15类路面要素矢量与实例标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路面要素提取与制图数据集在语义丰富度、数据模态多样性和场景复杂度上仍有不足，难以支撑细粒度路面制图研究。

Method: 先系统综述路面要素提取基准数据集（涵盖俯视—透视、大范围—局部、粗粒度—细粒度）及相关提取方法；针对上述缺口构建 WHU-Road3D：融合 MLS点云、全景影像、车辆轨迹，提供矢量化标注与逐点实例标注，覆盖15类路面要素（道路边界、斑马线、车道分隔线、停止线等常见标线及10类方向符号）。

Result: 数据集规模约100 km，覆盖多样化的中国公路与城市场景；据作者所知为首个可支撑如此全面路面要素制图的多模态数据集，并为实例分割、全要素提取与制图提供基准方法及代码。

Conclusion: WHU-Road3D 可推动路面实例分割、全要素提取与制图研究，并可作为分层路网拓扑推理、地图更新与街景生成的数据基础，数据集与基准代码将公开。

Abstract: Accurately extracting and mapping road surface elements is crucial for applications such as high-definition maps, road asset management, and intelligent transportation systems. This study first provides a comprehensive review of the benchmark datasets for extracting and mapping road elements, encompassing overhead-to-perspective data, large-to-local scales, and coarse-to-fine details. Subsequently, we thoroughly review the relevant approaches for extracting road surface elements. However, we argue that existing datasets still require enhancements in semantic richness, data modality diversity, and scenario complexity. To bridge these gaps, this paper presents WHU-Road3D, a large-scale multimodal dataset for fine-grained road surface mapping to address these shortcomings. WHU-Road3D provides approximately 100 km of diverse Chinese highways and urban streets. It features a rich combination of MLS point clouds, associated panoramic images, vehicle trajectories, detailed vectorized annotations, and pointwise instance annotations for road surface elements. The dataset carefully annotates 15 distinct categories of road elements, including common markings like road boundaries, zebra crossings, lane dividers, stop lines, as well as 10 types of direction symbols. To our knowledge, WHU-Road3D stands as the first multimodal dataset for mapping such a comprehensive range of road surface elements, designed to facilitate cutting-edge research in instance segmentation, full-element extraction and mapping on road surfaces. Beyond these immediate applications, WHU-Road3D also provides a valuable source for hierarchical road topology reasoning, map updating, and street scene generation. The dataset, along with the code for our benchmark methods, will be made publicly available at \href{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}.

</details>


### [105] [EvoMem: Self-Evolving Skills for Time-Aware Long Video Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25889)
*Yutong Guan,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Bing Sun,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMem：以自演化检索技能引导的时间感知长视频记忆智能体，通过层级时间索引记忆、状态驱动检索控制与奖励引导的技能演化，在四个长视频问答基准上平均达60.1%准确率，较此前最优方法平均提升8.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型LVLM的长视频检索仍依赖全局语义匹配，缺乏状态驱动的时间控制，常返回碎片化或冗余证据，难以维持连贯的证据链；长视频问答中还存在检索目标不清、记忆访问路径不当、过早终止等反复出现的流程性错误。

Method: 1) 构建层级时间索引记忆，将不同类型证据对齐到统一视频时间线；2) 检索时把已获证据的时间线索转化为显式信号，约束后续记忆访问；3) 引入"检索技能"提供过程级指导，使智能体依当前证据状态动态调整检索内容、记忆操作与目标时间区间，并决定何时停止；4) 借鉴强化学习，将技能演化建模为奖励引导的策略改进：每个候选技能作为外部策略补丁，依据验证集上答案正确性的状态转移分配奖励，接受高效技能、拒绝低质技能，且不更新模型参数。
